    "And so on" means we don't know, we need to study the situation. Legislation changes in time and the European Union is probably the most bureaucratic institution on Earth.

    We are a B2B Company, we don't work with private persons.

    Incoterms
    A. For all types of transport:
    EXW FCA CPT CIP DAF DDU DDP
    B. For maritime transport:
    FAS FOB CFR CIF DES DEQ DAT
    C. For rail or air transport:
    FCA

    Note: We will avoid rail transport for now.
    For international transport between EU and non-EU countries, wood boxes, crates and pallets need ISPM 15 phytosanitary treatment.
    Routes are subject to change without prior notice.
    After contracts are signed, the clients must send upfront payment so we can buy standards to verify the declared parameters by suppliers and laboratory tests results.
    Please note that we will not refund you the payment to buy standards for any reason and we will not send you a copy or share the standards with any other organization or person.
    It's forbidden to share standards.     [ Click here to see ASRO answer ]

    61211 - Grains, Beans and Oleaginous Fruits Open
    Download as PDF

    INFO FOR SUPPLIERS:

    [ Soil ] - Note: The soil must be fertile and according to needs of each crop. ( Do not plant wheat in shady areas or on sandy soils used for vineyards. You will waste energy and money. )

    [ Soil ] - Note: It's forbidden to deforest land to make room for farm land.

    [ Soil ] - Note: The soil will need to be clean and not contaminated. ( The land must not be close to cities, industrial zones, quarries, mines, garbage dumps or other polluted areas. The land needs to be inspected and tested. You need to have Soil Laboratory Tests Results. )

    [ Seeds ] - Note: The seeds used by farmers must be certified by accredited organizations - institutions.

    [ Sustainability ] - Note: We prefer suppliers that diversify their gardens-farms with varieties instead of cultivars. Cultivars are more vulnerable to disease. We like suppliers that build sustainable ecosystems.

    [ Sustainability ] - Note: Priority is given to suppliers that prevent desertification.

    [ Sustainability ] - Note: Products obtained in greenhouses heated with wood or polluting sources are not accepted.

    [ Water ] - Note: The water must come from sustainable sources. The water used to irrigate or to clean the products must not be contaminated. Suppliers must not use chlorinated or toxic water to irrigate. ( Industrial, Laboratory, Swimming Pool, Tap Water without treatmeant is not allowed. )

    [ Water ] - Note: It's forbidden to use Nuclear Reactor Cooling or Heavy Water even if treated.

    [ Water ] - Note: To clean the products suppliers must use only potable chlorinated tap water or potable water from wells. ( CXG 100-2023 )

    [ Water ] - Note: Suppliers must have document proof that the water used is clean and that they have the right to use the water to irrigate and/or to clean the products. ( e.g. Utility Bill, Contract with Water Pumping Station, Land Ownership or Land Rent Documents, Water Laboratory Tests Results )

    [ Fertilizers ] - Note: The fertilizers used must be preferably organic and not synthetic . If it's organic it must not be of human origin. If it's synthetic it must be from a sustainable source and eco friendly. ( You need to have Fertilizers Laboratory Tests Results. )

    [ Pesticides ] - Note: Suppliers are allowed to use Repellent Plants and Local Animal Predators to repel pests. ( You can even use Recorded Sounds of predators. )

    [ Pesticides ] - Note: The Pesticides must be traceable and approved by International and National Institutions. ( Don't throw away the packaging, take pictures of packaging for proof )

    [ Pesticides ] - Note: Suppliers are not allowed to use pesticides without recommendations from a Local Phytosanitary Institution via Public Bulletin / Notice.

    [ Pesticides ] - Note: Suppliers must figure out solutions for how to farm the products without using pesticides. There are many farmers around the world that overspray crops with pesticides. Where possible, avoid pesticides.

    [ Pesticides and Fertilizers ] - Note: Suppliers need to have plans and monitoring documents for all pesticides and fertilizers they will use. ( e.g. On 10/11/2034 i sprayed my plants with 1000ml of Solution XYZ according to "Local Phytosanitary Institution" Bulletin Nr. 355 - 13/10/2034 )

    [ Pesticides and Fertilizers ] - Note: Do not throw packaging on land or in waters. ( Dispose of packaging properly )

    [ Growth Regulator ] - Note: Applying ethephon (2-chloroethylphosphonic acid), ethylene gas or other stimulating chemicals is forbidden, even if the chemicals are approved by countries around the world.

    [ Tools ] - Note: To farm the land, suppliers can use [ hand tools ] or [ horse and plow ] or [ high-tech equipment ]. The tools and equipment must be environmentally friendly and energy efficient. Governments around the world offer many subsidies and financing to farmers.

    [ Food Safety ] - Note: If suppliers sort, wash, clean, package, label and grade food products they are food operators not farmers. They must also have food safety agency documents. For European Union all equipment - machinary must be according to Regulation (EC) nr. 852/2004.

    [ Food Safety ] - Note: The products must have low humidity, minimum contaminants and no foreign smell.

    [ Food Safety ] - Note: The products must not be irradiated. ( e.g. Codex Stan 106-1983 )

    [ Food Safety ] - Note: Suppliers must have accredited laboratory test results as proof of safety issued by authorized government agencies for all products. ( Private laboratory test results are not accepted. If the products are poisoned intentionally, it's an act of war ! )

    INFO FOR CLIENTS:

    Note: For all products in this section, your company has to send the technical grading system / specs / quality parameters and standards.

    Note: Your company must be authorized to buy these products. ( We do not accept requests from speculators )

    Note: The client company must also specify in advance, before cultivation and harvest:

    a) The period when you need these products ( Some plants are seasonal, some are all year round e.g. check harvest calendars )

    b) The variaty you need from official plant variety databases-catalogues ( No GMO allowed ! ), optional: Send plant(s) DNA fingerprints and products samples

    c) The degree of ripeness for each plant variaty

    d) The calories content per 100g for food products


    * goods of group 011 (Cereals)

    100119 - Cereals; wheat and meslin, durum wheat, other than seed

    BSEI-100119-0001 Common name: Durum wheat - Binomial name: Triticum durum Desf. [ 1 ] - Graded - For Human Consumption

    Explanatory note BSEI-100119: Durum wheat is a drought tolerant plant, it has high demand in North Africa and other countries in the Mediterranean basin. People use it to make Semolina, Cus Cus, Pasta and other products that do not expire fast in regions with hot weather. Not all people can afford refrigerators in areas where there is scarcity of infrastructure, money, resources.

    [ 1 ] Description: [ Author: Desfontaines, René Louiche ] - [ Book: Flora atlantica (in Latin) Vol. 1 ] - [ Place of Publication: Paris ] - [ Publisher: Apud Blanchon ] - [ Pag. 114 ] - [ Year:1800 ]

    * goods of group 014 (Oilseeds and oleaginous fruits)

    070992 - Vegetables; olives, fresh or chilled

    BSEI-070992-0001 Common name: Table Olive - Binomial name: Olea europaea L. [ 27 ] - Not Cured [ Sundried ] - Graded - For Human Consumption - For Curing

    BSEI-070992-0002 Common name: Table Olive - Binomial name: Olea europaea L. [ 27 ] - Not Cured - Graded - For Human Consumption - For Curing

    BSEI-070992-0003 Common name: Olive - Binomial name: Olea europaea L. [ 27 ] - Not Cured [ Raw ] - Graded - For Human Consumption - For Extra Virgin Olive Oil Production

    BSEI-070992-0004 Common name: Olive - Binomial name: Olea europaea L. [ 27 ] - Not Cured [ Raw ] - Graded - For Human Consumption - For Virgin Olive Oil Production

    BSEI-070992-0005 Common name: Olive - Binomial name: Olea europaea L. [ 27 ] - Not Cured [ Raw ] - Graded - Not For Human Consumption - For Lampante Olive Oil Production

    Explanatory note BSEI-070992: Olea europaea L. has many Synonyms. Scientists don't know yet what olives are cultivars and what olives are varieties. Table olives are big and have small pits compared to olives used for oil production. To see a list with cultivars / varieties check the EU variety register and Directive 2008/90/EC Article 7.

    [ 27 ] [ Author: Carl Linnaeus ] - [ Book: Species Plantarum (in Latin) Vol. 1 ] - [ Place of Publication: Stockholm ] - [ Publisher: Impensis Laurentii Salvii ] - [ Pag. 8 ] - [ Year:1753 ]

    Coconut with Endocarp - It has the shell

    Coconut with Exocarp and Mesocarp - It has the outer skin, the husk and the shell

    * goods of group 016 (Stimulant, spice and aromatic crops)

    090111 - Coffee; not roasted or decaffeinated

    BSEI-090111-0001 Common name: Green coffee [ Arabica 100% ] - Binomial name: Coffea arabica L. [ 54 ] - Graded - For Roasting

    BSEI-090111-0002 Common name: Green coffee [ Robusta 100% ] - Binomial name: Coffea canephora Pierre ex A.Froehner [ 55 ] - Graded - For Roasting

    Explanatory note BSEI-090111: The supplier must label and package the green coffee according to international standards and according to client specifications. The client company must send all the customs duties, excise duties and taxes upfront to the bank accounts specified by exporting and importing countries governments, there is no procedure because laws can change in time. No bean will leave the exporter's warehouse until all the taxes are paid to the exporting and importing countries. A few varieties you will find on worldcoffeeresearch.org.

    INFO FOR SUPPLIERS:

    [ Soil ] - Note: The soil must be fertile and according to needs of each crop. ( Do not plant wheat in shady areas or on sandy soils used for vineyards. You will waste energy and money. )

    [ Soil ] - Note: It's forbidden to deforest land to make room for farm land.

    [ Soil ] - Note: The soil will need to be clean and not contaminated. ( The land must not be close to cities, industrial zones, quarries, mines, garbage dumps or other polluted areas. The land needs to be inspected and tested. You need to have Soil Laboratory Tests Results. )

    [ Seeds ] - Note: The seeds used by farmers must be certified by accredited organizations - institutions.

    [ Sustainability ] - Note: We prefer suppliers that diversify their gardens-farms with varieties instead of cultivars. Cultivars are more vulnerable to disease. We like suppliers that build sustainable ecosystems.

    [ Sustainability ] - Note: Priority is given to suppliers that prevent desertification.

    [ Sustainability ] - Note: Products obtained in greenhouses heated with wood or polluting sources are not accepted.

    [ Water ] - Note: The water must come from sustainable sources. The water used to irrigate or to clean the products must not be contaminated. Suppliers must not use chlorinated or toxic water to irrigate. ( Industrial, Laboratory, Swimming Pool, Tap Water without treatmeant is not allowed. )

    [ Water ] - Note: It's forbidden to use Nuclear Reactor Cooling or Heavy Water even if treated.

    [ Water ] - Note: To clean the products suppliers must use only potable chlorinated tap water or potable water from wells. ( CXG 100-2023 )

    [ Water ] - Note: Suppliers must have document proof that the water used is clean and that they have the right to use the water to irrigate and/or to clean the products. ( e.g. Utility Bill, Contract with Water Pumping Station, Land Ownership or Land Rent Documents, Water Laboratory Tests Results )

    [ Fertilizers ] - Note: The fertilizers used must be preferably organic and not synthetic . If it's organic it must not be of human origin. If it's synthetic it must be from a sustainable source and eco friendly. ( You need to have Fertilizers Laboratory Tests Results. )

    [ Pesticides ] - Note: Suppliers are allowed to use Repellent Plants and Local Animal Predators to repel pests. ( You can even use Recorded Sounds of predators. )

    [ Pesticides ] - Note: The Pesticides must be traceable and approved by International and National Institutions. ( Don't throw away the packaging, take pictures of packaging for proof )

    [ Pesticides ] - Note: Suppliers are not allowed to use pesticides without recommendations from a Local Phytosanitary Institution via Public Bulletin / Notice.

    [ Pesticides ] - Note: Suppliers must figure out solutions for how to farm the products without using pesticides. There are many farmers around the world that overspray crops with pesticides. Where possible, avoid pesticides.

    [ Pesticides and Fertilizers ] - Note: Suppliers need to have plans and monitoring documents for all pesticides and fertilizers they will use. ( e.g. On 10/11/2034 i sprayed my plants with 1000ml of Solution XYZ according to "Local Phytosanitary Institution" Bulletin Nr. 355 - 13/10/2034 )

    [ Pesticides and Fertilizers ] - Note: Do not throw packaging on land or in waters. ( Dispose of packaging properly )

    [ Growth Regulator ] - Note: Applying ethephon (2-chloroethylphosphonic acid), ethylene gas or other stimulating chemicals is forbidden, even if the chemicals are approved by countries around the world.

    [ Tools ] - Note: To farm the land, suppliers can use [ hand tools ] or [ horse and plow ] or [ high-tech equipment ]. The tools and equipment must be environmentally friendly and energy efficient. Governments around the world offer many subsidies and financing to farmers.

    [ Food Safety ] - Note: If suppliers sort, wash, clean, package, label and grade food products they are food operators not farmers. They must also have food safety agency documents. For European Union all equipment - machinary must be according to Regulation (EC) nr. 852/2004.

    [ Food Safety ] - Note: The products must have low humidity, minimum contaminants and no foreign smell.

    [ Food Safety ] - Note: The products must not be irradiated. ( e.g. Codex Stan 106-1983 )

    [ Food Safety ] - Note: Suppliers must have accredited laboratory test results as proof of safety issued by authorized government agencies for all products. ( Private laboratory test results are not accepted. If the products are poisoned intentionally, it's an act of war ! )

    [ Documents ] - Note: In European Union most live plants need a plant passport before they are put on the market

    61219 - Agricultural Raw Materials Open
    Download as PDF

    Note: For all products in class [ 0195 ], your company has to send the technical specs / quality parameters

    Note: Your company must be authorized to buy these products. ( We do not accept requests from speculators )

    Note: Suppliers must have accredited laboratory test results as proof of quality

    Note: TSNR must be accompanied by a test certificate, issued by the competent authorities of the producing country, specifying the grade, specifications and test results of the rubber.

    Note: TSR = Technically Specified Rubber

    Note: e.g. 3 - 3L = both grade L and grade 3L and all grades between L and 3L, is considered to be grade 3L.

    Note: The client company must specify the variaty they need from official plant variety databases-catalogues ( No GMO allowed ! )

    Note: The seeds used by farmers must be certified.

    Note: The water used to irrigate or to clean the products must not be contaminated. The suppliers must have document proof that the water used is clean. The water must come from sustainable sources.

    Note: To farm the land, suppliers can use [ hand tools ] or [ horse and plow ] or [ high-tech equipment ]. The tools and equipment must be environmentally friendly and energy efficient. Governments around the world offer many subsidies and financing to farmers.

    Note: Suppliers must figure out solutions for how to farm the products without using pesticides. There are many farmers around the world that overspray crops with pesticides. Where possible, avoid pesticides.

    Note: Applying ethephon (2-chloroethylphosphonic acid), ethylene gas or other stimulating chemicals is forbidden, even if the chemicals are approved by countries around the world.

    Note: For all products in group [ 022 ], [ 023 ] and class [ 0291 ] your company has to send the technical specs / quality parameters

    Note: Your company must be authorized to buy these products. ( We do not accept requests from speculators )

    Note: Suppliers must have accredited laboratory test results as proof of safety issued by authorized government agencies for all products. ( Private laboratory test results are not accepted. If the products are poisoned intentionally, it's an act of war ! )

    Note: Suppliers for products in group [ 022 ], [ 023 ] and class [ 0291 ] must be authorized by food safety organizations, they must have proof of HACCP plan or HACCP accredited certificate

    Note: Some of the products in group [ 022 ], [ 023 ] and class [ 0291 ] must be pasteurized or sterilized and packaged according to food safety standards in recyclable or biodegradable food grade packaging

    Note: Frozen* means between -20 and -18° C, Cold* means between 2 and 4° C, Cool* means between 8 and 15° C, Room temperature* means between 15 and 20° C

    Note: The relative humidity (RH) must be under or equal to maximum allowed by exporter-importer governments, the products must be transported and stored according to manufacturer's instructions

    Note: Exporting - importing meat and animal byproducts is very complex. e.g. Only a few countries are allowed to import meat and animal byproducts inside the EU

    Note: We do not accept suppliers from regions with disease outbreaks. ( That means if you have diseases like Covid, Ebola virus, Swine flu, Mad Cow disease in your region we will not accept any meat or animal origin products from your company, even if your government tells us there is no problem, we must verify. )

    Note: If suppliers sort, wash, clean, package, label and grade food products they are food operators not farmers. They must also have food safety agency documents, for European Union all equipment - machinary must be according to Regulation (EC) nr. 852/2004.

    * goods of class 0291 (Natural honey)

    BSEI-040900-0001 Room temperature* - Acacia bee honey - for human consumption ( Apis mellifera )

    BSEI-040900-0002 Room temperature* - Polyflora bee honey - for human consumption ( Apis mellifera )

    BSEI-040900-0003 Room temperature* - Linden bee honey - for human consumption ( Apis mellifera )

    Note: For all products in class [ 0294 ] your company has to send the technical specs / quality parameters

    Note: Your company must be authorized to buy these products. ( We do not accept requests from speculators )

    Note: Suppliers must have accredited laboratory test results as proof of safety issued by authorized government agencies for all products. ( Private laboratory test results are not accepted. If the products are poisoned intentionally, it's an act of war ! )

    Note: For all products in class [ 0294 ], suppliers must have animal origin by-products safety documents

    Note: Fibers from cloned animals are not accepted

    Note: The animal shearer should sober and professional. No amatures should be allowed to shear the animals. Fibers from wounded animals are not accepted.

    Note: Suppliers must have traceability documents for all the products they used to treat the animals for lice, flies, maggots, mites, scab and other parasites.

    Note: Suppliers must have origin documents for all the products. ( e.g. It's forbidden to export goat hair from a region that has Ebola virus epidemic )

    Note: Before we buy the products, we must verify if suppliers use child labor, if they respect work labor laws, if suppliers pay salaries on time and so on.

    Note: Suppliers should use natural non-toxic insect repellents to counter damage from insects. ( e.g. Lavender and/or thyme herbal sachets )

    Note: The client company must specify the grading system, most countries have their own grading methods.

    Note: The fibers must be from the same breeds and it must have same color. No mixed colors and/or breeds and/or crossbreeds fibers are permited. ( Do not mix the fibers ! )

    Note: The fibers must be from same region of the animal. ( e.g. Wool from the belly region of the animal must not be mixed with lower quality wool from around the tail )

    Note: The fibers must have similar length. The suppliers must not mix long fibers with short fibers.

    Note: The suppliers must not try to fake wool, animal hair, silk or other expensive natural fibers. The suppliers must not mix wool with goat hair or dog hair or synthetic fibers and so on.

    Note: The products must have recyclable protective packaging against heat, sunlight, moisture and insects.

    Note: Wool and animal hair must be shorn.

    Note: The products must be labeled and baled according to client company specifications with recyclable and eco-friendly materials.

    Note: The practice of mulesing and treating animals with liquid nitrogen is forbbiden. The animals must not be mistreated. The animals must be well taken care of.

    Note: Suppliers must not brand the animals. The owners should use ear tags.

    Note: Suppliers must be washed and clean when they will shear the animals so they do not contaminate the products. No dogs allowed near the shearing area.

    Note: For all products in group [ 032 ], your company has to send the technical specs / quality parameters

    Note: Your company must be authorized to buy these products. ( We do not accept requests from speculators )

    Note: Suppliers must have accredited laboratory test results as proof of safety issued by authorized government agencies for all products. ( Private laboratory test results are not accepted. If the products are poisoned intentionally, it's an act of war ! )

    Note: The client company must specify the variaty they need from official plant variety databases-catalogues ( No GMO allowed ! )

    Note: The client company must specify the degree of ripeness for each product.

    Note: The client company must specify in advance, before harvest, the period when they need these products. ( Some plants are seasonal, some are all year round e.g. check harvest calendars )

    Note: The seeds used by farmers must be certified.

    Note: The water used to irrigate or to clean the products must not be contaminated. The suppliers must have document proof that the water used is clean. The water must come from sustainable sources.

    Note: To farm the land suppliers, can use [ hand tools ] or [ horse and plow ] or [ high-tech equipment ]. The tools and equipment must be environmentally friendly and energy efficient. Governments around the world offer many subsidies and financing to farmers.

    Note: Suppliers must figure out solutions for how to farm the products without using pesticides. There are many farmers around the world that overspray crops with pesticides. Where possible, avoid pesticides.

    Note: If suppliers sort, wash, clean, package, label and grade food products they are food operators not farmers. They must also have food safety agency documents, for European Union all equipment - machinary must be according to Regulation (EC) nr. 852/2004.

    Note: For all products in group [ 213 ], [ 214 ], your company has to send the technical specs / quality parameters

    Note: Your company must be authorized to buy these products. ( We do not accept requests from speculators )

    Note: Suppliers must have accredited laboratory test results as proof of safety issued by authorized government agencies for all products. ( Private laboratory test results are not accepted. If the products are poisoned intentionally, it's an act of war ! )

    Note: Suppliers for products in group [ 213 ], [ 214 ] must be authorized by food safety organizations, they must have proof of HACCP plan or HACCP accredited certificate

    Note: Products in group [ 213 ], [ 214 ] must be pasteurized or sterilized and packaged according to food safety standards in recyclable or biodegradable food grade packaging

    Note: Frozen* means between -20 and -18° C, Cold* means between 0 and 4° C, Cool* means between 8 and 15° C, Room temperature* means between 15 and 20° C

    Note: The relative humidity (RH) must be under or equal to maximum allowed by exporter-importer governments, the products must be transported and stored according to manufacturer's instructions

    Note: Products in this category must be pasteurized or sterilized and packaged according to food safety standards in recyclable or biodegradable food grade packaging

    Note: If suppliers sort, wash, clean, package, label and grade food products they are food operators not farmers. They must also have food safety agency documents, for European Union all equipment - machinary must be according to Regulation (EC) nr. 852/2004.

    BSEI-071120-0022 Room temperature* - Fresh natural whole olives without the stem in 6% brine - unsuitable for immediate consumption ( Olea europaea - Nyons / Tanche - Region Côte d'Azur - France )

    BSEI-071120-0026 Room temperature* - Fresh natural whole olives without the stem in 6% brine - unsuitable for immediate consumption ( Olea europaea - Mission - Region California, USA )

    Explanatory note for BSEI-071120: The fresh olives will be provisionally preserved according to Standard CXS 66-1981 in brine. Clients need to wait for olives to cure before they can use them. The brine is a solution of potable water and food-grade salt (CXS 150-1985) with zero additives. Supplies must also have document proof that the water is "drinking - potable water". Suppliers can get the public drinking water lab test from the local water company or from the Mayor's office. When the food agency of the country where the products are imported tests the brine and if they discover that the water is not drinking water, the supplier will not get payed and his products will be confiscated and destroyed. All suppliers must have legal documents proof that shows their products are safe.If the border guards consider that the olives are cured, they will classify the goods as 200570

    200950 - Juice; tomato, unfermented, not containing added spirit, whether or not containing added sugar or other sweetening matter

    61222 - Dairy products, Edible Oils and Fats Open
    Download as PDF

    Note: For all products in group [ 215 ], [ 216 ], [ 219 ], [ 221 ], [ 222 ], your company has to send the technical specs / quality parameters

    Note: Your company must be authorized to buy these products. ( We do not accept requests from speculators )

    Note: Suppliers must have accredited laboratory test results as proof of safety issued by authorized government agencies for all products. ( Private laboratory test results are not accepted. If the products are poisoned intentionally, it's an act of war ! )

    Note: Products in group [ 221 ], [ some products in 222 ] must be pasteurized or sterilized and packaged according to food safety standards in recyclable or biodegradable food grade packaging

    Note: Suppliers for products in this group must be authorized by food safety organizations, they must have proof of HACCP plan or HACCP accredited certificate

    Note: Frozen* means between -20 and -18° C, Cold* means between 0 and 4° C, Cool* means between 8 and 15° C, Room temperature* means between 15 and 20° C

    Note: The relative humidity (RH) must be under or equal to maximum allowed by exporter-importer governments, the products must be transported and stored according to manufacturer's instructions

    Note: Exporting - importing meat and animal byproducts is very complex. e.g. Only a few countries are allowed to import meat and animal byproducts inside the EU

    Note: We do not accept suppliers from regions with disease outbreaks. ( That means if you have diseases like Covid, Ebola virus, Swine flu, Mad Cow disease in your region we will not accept any meat or animal origin products from your company, even if your government tells us there is no problem, we must verify. )

    Note: If suppliers sort, wash, clean, package, label and grade food products they are food operators not farmers. They must also have food safety agency documents, for European Union all equipment - machinary must be according to Regulation (EC) nr. 852/2004.

    According to ISO 150:

    Raw linseed oil - oil obtained solely from mature seeds of linseed (Linum usitatissimum L.)

    Acid-refined linseed oil - oil obtained by refining raw linseed oil with acid

    Alkali-refined linseed oil - oil obtained by refining raw linseed oil with sodium hydroxide or other alkali solution

    Boiled linseed oil - oil obtained by incorporating driers in raw linseed oil or refined linseed oil and heating either alone orwhile blowing through air or oxygen

    Explanatory note for BSEI-040610, BSEI-040640, BSEI-040690: Company client - Wait a minute, why should i buy cheese from Europe ? i can make my own European cheese. I just need the recipe. To obtain the same taste, you need the grass, the animals, the rennet, the water, the air from these countries, also the skilled workers. Geographical indications establish intellectual property rights for specific products, whose qualities are specifically linked to the area of production.

    61223 - Meat, Poultry and Game Open
    Download as PDF

    Note: For all products in this section, your company has to send the technical specs / quality parameters

    Note: Your company must be authorized to buy these products. ( We do not accept requests from speculators )

    Note: Suppliers must have accredited laboratory test results as proof of safety issued by authorized government agencies for all products. ( Private laboratory test results are not accepted. If the products are poisoned intentionally, it's an act of war ! )

    Note: Suppliers for products in this section must be authorized by food safety organizations, they must have proof of HACCP plan or HACCP accredited certificate

    Note: Some of the products in this section must be pasteurized or sterilized and packaged according to food safety standards in recyclable or biodegradable food grade packaging

    Note: Frozen* means between -20 and -18° C, Cold* means between 2 and 4° C, Cool* means between 8 and 15° C, Room temperature* means between 15 and 20° C

    Note: The relative humidity (RH) must be under or equal to maximum allowed by exporter-importer governments, the products must be transported and stored according to manufacturer's instructions

    Note: Exporting - importing meat and animal byproducts is very complex. e.g. Only a few countries are allowed to import meat and animal byproducts inside the EU

    Note: We do not accept suppliers from regions with disease outbreaks. ( That means if you have disease like Covid, Ebola virus, Swine flu, Mad Cow disease in your region we will not accept any meat or animal origin products from your company, even if your government tells us there is no problem, we must verify. )

    Note: For all products in this category you must have documents that you raised, feed, slaughter, proccesed the animals according to international food safety - food hygiene - biosecurity standards. The animals can be slaughter and stunned or the client company must send their own butchers to slaughter the animals according to their religios believes e.g. Kosher, Halal and so on. Universal Declaration of Human Rights Article 18 - Everyone has the right to freedom of thought, conscience and religion.

    Note: We do not accept meat from sick or infected animals, animals allowed to eat garbage, abused or bruised or injured animals, newly born, pregnant animals, animals with calfs under 1 year, animals with high fat deposits, stressed animals, uncastrated male animals, castrated under 4 months male animals, not well rested animals during the 24-hour period before slaughter, animals handled with electric prodders, animals living in unhygienic and overcrowded conditions, live animals not transported in clean-disinfected-washed-ventilated specialized vehicles, live animals transported in overcrowded vehicles, live animals transported more then 50-100km from place of origin.

    Note: The meat must be washed only with potable water, the meat should not have blood marks and must be clean, the meat must not have any foreign smell, the meat must not have broken bones, the meat must not be bruised, the meat must not be discolored, the meat must have characteristic color and so on, the meat must have the mandatory food safety and legal documents according to the international legislation and national legislation of each country.

    Note: Suppliers must eviscerate and clean the products with potable water, before they put them on scale to be weighted. They will not be payed for the eviscerated content - impurities.

    Note: Suppliers must have contracts with authorised companies to buy and/or to dispose the eviscerated content - impurities.

    Note: Suppliers must have documents evidence that the products do not contain antibiotics, parasites - pathogens, heavy metals, toxic chemicals.

    Note: Suppliers must have documents evidence that the products are not radioactive and that they do not come from contaminated areas like Chernobyl.

    Note: The products must be graded according to importer's country legislation and client needs.

    Note: Suppliers are not allowed to mix fresh products with old and/or spoiled products.

    Note: Meat is considered a biological weapon. Before we release any payment for these products, we want the legal representative of the supplier, his wife, his kids and his family to cook and eat a meat sample from the same lot that is shipped to clients around the world.

    Note: If suppliers sort, wash, clean, package, label and grade food products they are food operators not farmers. They must also have food safety agency documents, for European Union all equipment - machinary must be according to Regulation (EC) nr. 852/2004.

If you have a restaurant and if you need fresh non-frozen meat your company needs to be close to a slaughterhouse also. Transporting fresh non-frozen meat long distance is not efficient. When they will invent commercial vehicles that run on free electricity then we can transport Cold* meat long distance.

    Explanatory note for BSEI-160220, BSEI-160241, BSEI-160249, BSEI-160100: The supplier must respect the manufacturing rules - normes - procedures. Additives are not allowed.

    Close Window
    61224 - Fish and Seafood Open
    Download as PDF

    Note: For all products in this section, your company has to send the technical specs / quality parameters

    Note: Your company must be authorized to buy these products. ( We do not accept requests from speculators )

    Note: Suppliers must have accredited laboratory test results as proof of safety issued by authorized government agencies for all products. ( Private laboratory test results are not accepted. If the products are poisoned intentionally, it's an act of war ! )

    Note: Suppliers for products in this section must be authorized by food safety organizations, they must have proof of HACCP plan or HACCP accredited certificate

    Note: Some of the products in this section must be pasteurized or sterilized and packaged according to food safety standards in recyclable or biodegradable food grade packaging

    Note: Frozen* means between -20 and -18° C, Cold* means between 2 and 4° C, Cool* means between 8 and 15° C, Room temperature* means between 15 and 20° C

    Note: The relative humidity (RH) must be under or equal to maximum allowed by exporter-importer governments, the products must be transported and stored according to manufacturer's instructions

    Note: Exporting - importing meat and animal byproducts is very complex. e.g. Only a few countries are allowed to import meat and animal byproducts inside the EU

    Note: We do not accept suppliers from regions with disease outbreaks. ( That means if you have disease like Covid, Ebola virus, Swine flu, Mad Cow disease in your region we will not accept any meat or animal origin products from your company, even if your government tells us there is no problem, we must verify. )

    Note: For all products in this category you must have documents that you raised, feed, slaughter, proccesed the animals according to international food safety - food hygiene - biosecurity standards. The animals can be slaughter according to the importer's country food safety laws or the client company must send their own butchers to slaughter the animals according to their religios believes e.g. Kosher, Halal and so on. Universal Declaration of Human Rights Article 18 - Everyone has the right to freedom of thought, conscience and religion.

    Note: We do not accept meat from sick or infected animals, animals allowed to eat garbage, abused or bruised or injured animals, newly born, pregnant animals, animals with high fat deposits, stressed animals, not well rested animals during the 24-hour period before slaughter, animals handled with electric prodders, animals living in unhygienic and overcrowded conditions, live animals not transported in clean-disinfected-washed-ventilated specialized vehicles, live animals transported in overcrowded vehicles, live animals transported more then 50-100km from place of origin.

    Note: The meat must be washed only with potable water, the meat should not have blood marks and must be clean, the meat must not have any foreign smell, the meat must not have broken bones, the meat must not be bruised, the meat must not be discolored, the meat must have characteristic color and so on, the meat must have the mandatory food safety and legal documents according to the international legislation and national legislation of each country.

    Note: Suppliers must eviscerate and clean the products with potable water, before they put them on scale to be weighted. They will not be payed for the eviscerated content - impurities.

    Note: Suppliers must have contracts with authorised companies to buy and/or to dispose the eviscerated content - impurities.

    Note: Suppliers must have documents evidence that the products do not contain antibiotics, parasites - pathogens, heavy metals, toxic chemicals.

    Note: Suppliers must have documents evidence that the products are not radioactive and that they do not come from contaminated areas like Chernobyl.

    Note: The products must be graded according to importer's country legislation and client needs.

    Note: Suppliers are not allowed to mix fresh products with old and/or spoiled products. ( Suppliers must have documents evidence that the products are from today's catch, not from last week, that also means do not stock products until we have signed permission from clients to go at sea to capture the products. )

    Note: Meat is considered a biological weapon. Before we release any payment for these products, we want the legal representative of the supplier, his wife, his kids and his family to cook and eat a meat sample from the same lot that is shipped to clients around the world.

    Note: Suppliers must have documents evidence that the products are captured in season and not in time of fishing prohibition.

    Note: Suppliers must have documents evidence that they are allowed to capture the products and that they are not overfishing in international waters ( e.g. proof you didn't steal the fish from other countries territorial waters )

    Note: Suppliers must have documents evidence that the tools they used to capture the products are legal and sustainable.

    Note: Suppliers must have documents evidence that the water in the area where they captured - farmed the products is clean with zero pollution. ( No matter if the products come from fishing or aquaculture )

    Note: Suppliers must have documents evidence that the ice used to keep the products cold comes from potable water that is clean without any impurities.

    Note: Suppliers should use fishing boats without engines where possible. Trawling, poisons, dynamite, nets and traps is not sustainable.

    Note: Fresh products on ice can not be transported very far. You have a few days window to freeze them, eat them or to preserve them.

    Note: Buy the fishing products only if you really need them or for special occasions.

    Note: Suppliers must go at sea only when there is clear sky and sunny, depending on FAO region where they fish. ( Beaufort scale )

    Note: Suppliers must specify how to prepare, fillet, cook and eat the products.

    Note: Fish and seafood legal size, stocks and status will be checked before the contracts are signed. ( Laws, rules and regulations change all the time, today a product's legal size limit can be 25cm+, tomorrow 45cm+ and after tomorrow it can be on the endangered species list and so on. Imagine we are on a spaceship called Earth that is traveling in space. For the spaceship to reach it's destination we must use our resources wisely, else future generations will have nothing to eat. )

    Note: Suppliers must specify the level of glaze and the weight of fish before and after freezing.

    Note: If suppliers sort, wash, clean, package, label and grade food products they are food operators not farmers. They must also have food safety agency documents, for European Union all equipment - machinary must be according to Regulation (EC) nr. 852/2004.

    Note: Suppliers must have documents evidence that the water from the fishing - aquaculture is clean, not contaminated.

    Note: To verify EU commercial designations of fishery and aquaculture products click here.

    BSEI-050800-0005 Cnidaria - Anthozoa - Octocorallia - Scleralcyonacea [ Soft coral ] - Source: Aquaculture - For coral reefs restauration

    Explanatory note for BSEI-050800: Suppliers must have documents evidence that the source for corals is Aquaculture. Corals need CITES N - Reintroduction or introduction into the wild - permits also. The corals must be alive. Corals around the world are endangared do to climate change. If you want your children to eat fish in the future, you need to help with coral reefs restoration. Clients must specify the coral species name and location on the shallow-water or deep ocean seabed where they want the products to be delivered. After we verify with a marine biologist if location is ok, the corals will be delivered at specified location by clients.
